- **working-a-bead**: Router for the activity-shaped recipes. Takes a bead-id (and optional --recipe=&lt;name&gt; override), runs `bd show`, scores by bead.type + description heuristics, and dispatches to the matching `&lt;activity&gt;-a-bead` recipe. On a keyword tie (2+ recipes level at top score), breaks the tie by reading the bead text against the tied recipes' definitions, dispatches, and says in one line which it picked and why (--recipe=&lt;name&gt; overrides). Direct invocation of a specific recipe (`/bugfix-a-bead`, `/feature-a-bead`, etc.) still works and bypasses the router. - **workshop**: Collaborative draft work — read a piece, assess where it is in its development, ask one question if intent is unclear, then work in the direction the writer wants.
